<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/**</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * Small ghost trigger + menu used in agent composers: shows the selected</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">ComposerPickerMenu</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> {...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">props</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> /></span></span>
